- **gate-discipline-diagnose**: Diagnoses a failing CI gate, lint, or audit and fixes the artefact rather than weakening the gate. Use when a gate fails. The orchestrator converts findings into items in .work/active/ with gate_origin:docs and tags:[documentation]. Auto-triggers during /agile-workflow:release-deploy.
